INDUSTRY OVERVIEW 行業概覽
Though China achieved a 5% year-on-year (“YoY”) GDP growth, it 儘管中國的國內生產總值同比（「同比」）增長5%，
is evident that the Chinese economy experienced great difficulties 但顯然2024年中國經濟遭遇重重困難。主要挑戰之
in 2024. One of the major challenges is the continuous weakening 一是消費者信心持續疲軟，導致居民消費支出明顯
of consumer confidence that caused obvious decreases in 減少，即所謂的「消費降級」。
consumer spending, or so-called “consumption downgrade”.
This was attributable to multiple structural reasons, such as 此乃由多種結構性原因所致，如失業率高於預期、
higher-than-expected unemployment rate, demographically 從人口結構來看新生兒人數下降以及對家庭可支配
falling new-born numbers, and negative outlook on households’ 收入的負面展望。如同與大多數消費者相關行業的
disposable incomes. Like most of the consumer-related sectors, 情況類似，中國奶牛牧場運營商的經營環境及財務
the operating environments and financial performances of 表現既充滿挑戰又令人失望。

disappointing.
In 2024, the dairy industry in China was amongst the ten “profit 於2024年，中國乳製品行業已列入十大「利潤下滑
declining industries”. Almost all listed dairy companies reported 行業」榜單。幾乎所有上市的乳製品公司營收均出
revenue decline, and the sales revenue of all major categories of 現下滑，超高溫殺菌乳(UHT)、酸奶、奶酪、巴氏
dairy products, including ultra-high temperature, yogurt, cheese 殺菌奶等主要乳製品品類的銷售收入均出現下滑。
and pasteurized milk, experienced decline. Following a decrease 繼2023年下降2%後，乳製品總銷量進一步下降約
of 2% in 2023, the total sales volume of dairy products further 1.5%。
decreased by about 1.5%.
Starting from the third quarter of 2021, up to now, this round of 自2021年第三季度起至今，此輪全國性牛奶價格下
nationwide milk price decline has lasted for more than 3 years. 跌已持續逾3年。全國平均原料奶價格由2021年8月
The national average raw milk price dropped from RMB4.4/kg in 的人民幣4.4元╱公斤下降30%至2024年12月的人
August 2021, to RMB3.1/kg in December 2024, representing a 民幣3.1元╱公斤。

From second half of 2023 up till now, the supply side consolidation 自2023年下半年起至今，供給側整合一直持續。部

On the other hand, since 2023, quantities of imported dairy 另一方面，自2023年起，進口乳製品的數量大幅
products have been fallen significantly. In 2024, China imported 下降。2024年，中國進口各類乳製品2.6百萬噸，
2.6 million tons of various dairy products, representing a decrease 同比下降9.6%；進口總額為110億美元，同比下降
of 9.6% year on year. The total imported value was USD11 billion, 8.39%。同時，由於國內牛奶價格跌至低位，進口
representing a decrease of 8.39% year on year. Also, since the 乳製品失去價格優勢。預計中國乳製品進口量將繼
domestic milk price dropped to a low level, the imported dairy 續下降。

For the first time since 2018, the domestic raw milk production 自2018年以來，國內原料奶產量首次出現下降。
volume has experienced decrease. In 2024, total raw milk 2024年，原料奶總產量下降2.9%至約40.8百萬噸

million ton (2023: 42.0 million ton). It demonstrates the effects 帶來的影響。由於近期的整合，一旦目前的衰退期
from the reduction of supply side capacities. As a results of recent 結束，領先的運營商預計將在整體原料奶供應方面
consolidation, once the current downturn is over, the leading 擁有更強大的地位和更主導性的市場份額。
operators are expected to have much stronger position and more
dominant market share in terms of overall raw milk supply.
On the other hand, in 2024, the costs of feed continued to drop 另一方面，2024年，飼料成本繼續下降，部分關鍵
with prices of some of the key materials and ingredients becoming 原料及成分價格走低，因此緩解了2023年奶牛養殖
lower. This has eased the significant cost pressure that troubled 業面臨的巨大成本壓力。

2022 and effective from the Listing Date. The remaining life of the 經修訂，並於上市日期生效。於本年報日期，AAG
AAG PSP is about two months as at the date of this annual report. 績效股份計劃的剩餘年期約為兩個月。
The following is a summary of the principal terms of the AAG PSP: 以下為AAG績效股份計劃的主要條款概要：
1. Purpose of the AAG PSP 1. AAG績效股份計劃的目的
The purpose of the AAG PSP is to (i) foster an ownership AAG績效股份計劃的目的是(i)在本集團內培
culture within the Group which aligns the interests of the 養主人翁文化，使本集團高管及僱員的利益
executives and employees of the Group with the interests 與股東利益一致；(ii)激勵參與者實現本公司
of Shareholders, (ii) motivate participants to achieve key 及╱或其各自業務部門的關鍵財務及運營目
financial and operational goals of the Company and/or its 標；及(iii)使僱員薪酬總額具有足夠的競爭
respective business units and (iii) make total employee 力，以招聘及挽留具備相稱技能的員工，推
remuneration sufficiently competitive to recruit and 動本公司成為世界一流的公司。
retain staff having skills that are commensurate with the
Company’s ambition to become a world-class company.

of companies in the Group) subject to such employees 該等僱員須符合AAG績效股份計劃所載的相

determined by the Remuneration Committee of the Board. 標準。
The AAG PSP is subject to a maximum limit of 40 AAG績效股份計劃不時的參與人數上限為40
participants from time to time. 人。

Reasons for the adoption of the AAG Share Option Scheme 採納AAG股份期權計劃的理由
The AAG Share Option Scheme is intended to provide the AAG股份期權計劃旨在使本公司可靈活地向參與者
Company with a flexible means of giving incentive to, rewarding, 提供激勵、獎勵、酬謝、補償及╱或福利，以及達
remunerating, compensating and/or providing benefits to the 成董事會不時批准的其他目的，吸引技術熟練及經
Participants and for such other purposes as the Board may 驗豐富的人員，激勵彼等留任本集團，並通過向彼
approve from time to time, and attract skilled and experienced 等提供獲得本公司股本權益的機會，推動彼等為本
personnel, to incentivise them to remain with the Group and to 集團的未來發展及擴張而努力。
motivate them to strive for the future development and expansion
of the Group by providing them with the opportunity to acquire
equity interests in the Company.
Scope of Participants 參與者範圍
Participants of the AAG Share Option Scheme include (i) any AAG股份期權計劃的參與者包括(i)執行董事，或(ii)
executive Director, or (ii) any full-time employee of the Group 受僱於本集團超過12個月的本集團全職僱員（不論
who has been employed by the Group for more than 12 months 其是否為本集團內任何公司的董事）。經考慮參與者
(regardless of whether he is a director of any company within the 的範圍及AAG股份期權計劃的條款後，董事認為，
Group). Having considered the scope of the Participants and the 採納AAG股份期權計劃將使本公司能夠靈活地激勵
terms of the AAG Share Option Scheme, the Directors are of the 及獎勵僱員為其發展、增長及成功助力，從而達致
view that the adoption of the AAG Share Option Scheme would 本集團的長期發展目標。
give the Company flexibility to incentivize and reward employees
to contribute to the development, growth and success of the
Group, thus achieving the long-term growth targets of the Group.

1. Technical Service Agreement 1. 技術服務協議
Annona Technical Services Pte. Ltd. (“ATS”), a wholly- 佳發（本公司控股股東之緊密聯繫人）的全資
owned subsidiary of Japfa Ltd. (a close associate of the 附屬公司Annona Technical Services Pte.
controlling shareholder of the Company) entered into an Ltd（.「ATS」）與本公司於2022年12月14日
amended and restated technical services agreement (the 訂立經修訂及重列技術服務協議（「技術服務
“Technical Service Agreement”) with the Company dated 協議」），ATS已同意按正常商業條款及公平
14 December 2022, ATS has agreed to provide financial 協商的服務費（相當於成本加5%附加費，但
solutions (including assisting with certain technical aspects 受本集團應付ATS款項的年度上限規限），
of the financial statements and upgrading the financial 以成本分攤方式向本集團提供財務解決方案
management tools to review the Group’s business against （包括協助財務報表的某些技術方面及升級
key performance indicators), debt financing and debt risk 財務管理工具，從而根據關鍵績效指標審閱
solutions (including providing training to the Group’s staff 本集團的業務）、債務融資及債務風險解決
on financial, capital expenditure and budget planning, 方案（包括為本集團的員工提供財務、資本
developing balanced capital structure and assisting 支出及預算規劃方面的培訓、發展平衡的資

Continuous Professional Development of Directors 董事的持續專業發展
Our Directors generally keep themselves updated on new laws 我們的董事通常會隨時了解新法律法規以及行業及
and regulations as well as changes in the industry and general 總體經濟環境變化。如有需要，本公司則委聘外部

2.4 MATERIAL ACCOUNTING POLICIES 2.4 重大會計政策
Fair value measurement 公允價值計量
The Group measures its biological assets and equity 本集團於各報告期末計量其生物資產及指定
investment designated at fair value through other 為以公允價值計量且其變動計入其他全面收
comprehensive income at fair value at the end of each 益的股權投資。公允價值為市場參與者於計
reporting period. Fair value is the price that would be 量日期在有序交易中出售資產所收取或轉讓
received to sell an asset or paid to transfer a liability in 負債所支付的價格。公允價值計量乃基於假
an orderly transaction between market participants at the 設出售資產或轉讓負債的交易於資產或負債
measurement date. The fair value measurement is based 主要市場或（並無主要市場）於資產或負債最
on the presumption that the transaction to sell the asset 具優勢市場進行。本集團必須能夠進入該主
or transfer the liability takes place either in the principal 要或最具優勢市場。資產或負債的公允價值
market for the asset or liability, or in the absence of a 乃採用市場參與者為資產或負債定價時所用
principal market, in the most advantageous market for the 的假設計量，即假設市場參與者依照其最佳
asset or liability. The principal or the most advantageous 經濟利益行事。
market must be accessible by the Group. The fair value of
an asset or a liability is measured using the assumptions
that market participants would use when pricing the asset
or liability, assuming that market participants act in their
economic best interest.

Fair value measurement (continued) 公允價值計量（續）
A fair value measurement of a non-financial asset takes into 非金融資產的公允價值計量計入市場參與者
account a market participant’s ability to generate economic 通過使用該資產的最高及最佳用途或通過將
benefits by using the asset in its highest and best use or 該資產出售予將使用其最高及最佳用途的另
by selling it to another market participant that would use 一市場參與者而產生經濟利益的能力。
the asset in its highest and best use.
The Group uses valuation techniques that are appropriate 本集團使用在各情況下適用的估值技術，並
in the circumstances and for which sufficient data are 有足夠的數據可用於計量公允價值，以盡量
available to measure fair value, maximising the use of 增加使用相關可觀察輸入數據及盡量減少使
relevant observable inputs and minimising the use of 用不可觀察輸入數據。
unobservable inputs.
All assets and liabilities for which fair value is measured 於財務報表內計量或披露公允價值的所有資
or disclosed in the financial statements are categorised 產及負債，均根據對公允價值計量整體而言
within the fair value hierarchy, described as follows, based 屬重大的最低層級輸入數據，在下述公允價
on the lowest level input that is significant to the fair value 值層級內進行分類：
measurement as a whole:
Level 1 - based on quoted prices (unadjusted) in active 第一級－ 基於相同資產或負債於活躍市場
markets for identical assets or liabilities 的報價（未經調整）
Level 2 - based on valuation techniques for which the 第二級－ 基於對公允價值計量而言屬重大
lowest level input that is significant to the fair 的最低層級輸入數據可以直接或
value measurement is observable, either directly 間接觀察到的估值技術

are subsequently measured at amortised cost, using 按攤銷成本計量，惟倘貼現影響屬輕微則按

stated at cost. Gains and losses are recognised in the 認。

Derecognition of financial liabilities 終止確認金融負債
A financial liability is derecognised when the obligation 金融負債於負債的責任解除、取消或屆滿時
under the liability is discharged or cancelled, or expires. 終止確認。
When an existing financial liability is replaced by another 如果現有金融負債被另一項由同一貸款人提
from the same lender on substantially different terms, or the 供而絕大部分條款不同的負債所取代，或現
terms of an existing liability are substantially modified, such 有負債的條款經大幅修改，則有關取代或修
an exchange or modification is treated as a derecognition 改視為終止確認原有負債及確認新負債，而
of the original liability and a recognition of a new liability, 相關賬面值的差額會於綜合損益表內確認。
and the difference between the respective carrying
amounts is recognised in the consolidated statement of
profit or loss.
Offsetting of financial instruments 抵銷金融工具
Financial assets and financial liabilities are offset and the 在現時有可執行的合法權利抵銷已確認金額
net amount is reported in the statement of financial position 且有意按淨額基準結算，或同時變現資產及
if there is a currently enforceable legal right to offset the 償還負債的情況下，金融資產與金融負債方
recognised amounts and there is an intention to settle on 可抵銷，淨額則於財務狀況表呈報。
a net basis, or to realise the assets and settle the liabilities
simultaneously.
Treasury shares 庫存股份
Own equity instruments which are reacquired and held 由本公司或本集團重新收購及持有的自有權
by the Company or the Group (treasury shares) are 益工具（庫存股份）按成本直接於權益確認。
recognised directly in equity at cost. No gain or loss 概無就購買、出售、發行或註銷本集團自有
is recognised in the statement of profit or loss on the 權益工具於損益表確認收益或虧損。
purchase, sale, issue or cancellation of the Group’s own
equity instruments.
Inventories 存貨
Inventories mainly include raw materials, low-value 存貨主要包括原材料、低值消耗品及成品
consumables and finished goods. Inventories are stated 奶。存貨按成本及可變現淨值兩者中的較低
at the lower of cost and net realisable value. Cost is 者入賬。成本乃按加權平均基準確定，而就
determined on the weighted average basis and, in the case 在製品與成品奶而言，成本包括直接原材
of work in progress and finished goods, comprises direct 料、直接勞動力及適當比例的經常性開支。
materials, direct labour and an appropriate proportion of 可變現淨值則按預計售價減完成及出售時所
overheads. Net realisable value is based on estimated 產生的任何估計成本確定。
selling prices less any estimated costs to be incurred to
completion and disposal.

of financial position comprise cash on hand and at banks, 手頭現金及銀行存款，以及可隨時轉換為已

less bank overdrafts which are repayable on demand and 本集團現金管理一部分的銀行透支。

Provisions 撥備
A provision is recognised when a present obligation (legal 如果本集團因過往事件須承擔現時的責任

it is probable that a future outflow of resources will be 來資源外流，且該責任涉及金額能夠可靠估
required to settle the obligation, provided that a reliable 計，則確認撥備。
estimate can be made of the amount of the obligation.

reimbursed, the reimbursement is recognised as a separate 該補償被確認為一項獨立資產，惟僅於補償
asset, but only when the reimbursement is virtually certain. 幾乎確定時方會予以確認。經減去任何補償
The expense relating to a provision is presented in the 後的撥備相關開支於損益表中呈列。
statement of profit or loss net of any reimbursement.
When the effect of discounting is material, the amount 如果貼現影響重大，則確認為撥備的金額將
recognised for a provision is the present value at the end 為報告期末預期須用作履行責任的未來開支
of the reporting period of the future expenditures expected 現值。貼現現值隨著時間增加的金額計入綜
to be required to settle the obligation. The increase in the 合損益表的融資成本。
discounted present value amount arising from the passage
of time is included in finance costs in the consolidated
statement of profit or loss.

inputs (such as market interest rates) when available and is 立信貸評級）。
required to make certain entity-specific estimates (such as
the subsidiary’s stand-alone credit rating).

Estimation uncertainty (continued) 估計不確定因素（續）
Estimation of fair value of dairy cows and beef cattle 奶牛及肉牛的公允價值估算
The Group’s dairy cows and beef cattle are measured 本集團的奶牛及肉牛按公允價值減銷售成本
at fair value less costs to sell. The fair value of dairy 計算。奶牛及肉牛的公允價值根據各報告期
cows and beef cattle is determined based on either the 末的市場定價（經參考奶牛的品種、年齡、
market-determined prices as at the end of each reporting 生長條件、所產生的成本及預期產奶量調整
period adjusted with reference to the species, age, 以反映奶牛的特徵及╱或生長階段的差異）
growing condition, cost incurred and expected yield of 釐定；或當無法獲得市場定價時，則根據奶
the milk to reflect differences in characteristics and/or 牛及肉牛產生的預期淨現金流量的現值按當
stages of growth of dairy cows; or the present value of 前市場利率貼現釐定。估計發生的任何變動
expected net cash flows from the dairy cows and beef 均可能對奶牛及肉牛的公允價值產生重大影
cattle discounted at a current market-determined rate, 響。獨立合資格專業估值師和管理層定期審
when market-determined prices are unavailable. Any 查假設和估計，以確定奶牛及肉牛的公允價
changes in the estimates may affect the fair value of the 值的任何重大變化。詳情載於財務報表附註
dairy cows and beef cattle significantly. The independent 20。
qualified professional valuer and management review the
assumptions and estimates periodically to identify any
significant change in fair value of dairy cows and beef
cattle. Further details are given in note 20 to the financial
statements.
Impairment of non-financial assets (other than goodwill) 非金融資產減值（商譽除外）
The Group assesses whether there are any indicators 本集團於各報告期末評估所有非金融資產
of impairment for all non-financial assets (including the （包括使用權資產）是否有任何減值跡象。
right-of-use assets) at the end of each reporting period. 未確定使用年期的無形資產每年進行減值測
Indefinite life intangible assets are tested for impairment 試及在其他存在有關減值跡象時進行測試。
annually and at other times when such an indicator exists. 其他非金融資產於有跡象顯示未必能收回賬
Other non-financial assets are tested for impairment when 面值時進行減值測試。當資產或現金產生單
there are indicators that the carrying amounts may not 位的賬面值超出其可收回金額（即其公允價
be recoverable. An impairment exists when the carrying 值減銷售成本與其使用價值兩者之較高者）
value of an asset or a cash-generating unit exceeds its 時，即存在減值。公允價值減銷售成本的
recoverable amount, which is the higher of its fair value 計算乃基於從類似資產於公平交易中的具約
less costs of disposal and its value in use. The calculation 束力銷售交易的可用數據或可觀察的市場價
of the fair value less costs of disposal is based on available 格減出售資產的增量成本。當計算使用價值
data from binding sales transactions in an arm’s length 時，管理層須估計來自該資產或現金產生單
transaction of similar assets or observable market prices 位的預期未來現金流量，並選用合適的貼現
less incremental costs for disposing of the asset. When 率以計算該等現金流量的現值。
value in use calculations are undertaken, management
must estimate the expected future cash flows from the
asset or cash-generating unit and choose a suitable
discount rate in order to calculate the present value of
those cash flows.

4. OPERATING SEGMENT INFORMATION 4. 經營分部資料
For management purposes, the Group is organised into 就管理而言，本集團根據其產品和服務分為
business units based on their products and services and 多個業務部門，並具有以下三個可報告經營
has three reportable operating segments as follows: (1) 分部：(1)生產及銷售原料奶的原料奶業務；
raw milk business for the production and sale of raw milk; (2)飼養及銷售肉牛的肉牛業務；及(3)銷售
(2) beef cattle business for raising and sale of beef cattle; 乳製品、飼料產品及其他的其他業務。
and (3) ancillary business for sales of milk products, feed
products and others.
Management monitors the results of the Group’s operating 管理層獨立監察本集團各經營分部的業績，
segments separately for the purpose of making decisions 以作出有關資源分配及表現評估的決定。分
about resource allocation and performance assessment. 部表現乃根據可報告分部利潤╱虧損（其為
Segment performance is evaluated based on reportable 對經調整稅前利潤╱虧損的一種計量）予以
segment profit/loss, which is a measure of adjusted profit/ 評估。經調整稅前利潤╱虧損與本集團稅前
loss before tax. The adjusted profit/loss before tax is 利潤的計量保持一致，惟該等計量不包括其
measured consistently with the Group’s profit before tax 他收入及開支、本集團金融工具產生的非租
except that other income and expenses, non-lease-related 賃相關融資成本、長期應收款項減值以及總
finance costs from the Group’s financial instruments, 辦事處或公司行政開支。
impairment of long-term receivable as well as head office
or corporate administrative expenses are excluded from
such measurement.
Intersegment sales and transfers are transacted with 分部間銷售及轉讓乃參考以當時的現行市場
reference to the selling prices used for sales made to third 價格向第三方銷售所採用的售價進行交易。
parties at the then prevailing market prices.

The Group is exposed to a number of risks related to its 本集團面臨一系列與生物資產有關的風險。
biological assets. The Group is exposed to the following 本集團面臨以下風險：
risks:
i. Regulatory and environmental risks i. 監管及環境風險
The Group is subject to laws and regulations in the 本集團須遵守其經營種植業和養殖業
location in which its plantation and breeding take 業務所在地的法律法規。本集團已制
place. The Group has established environmental 定環境政策及程序，旨在遵守地方環
policies and procedures aimed at complying with 境及其他法律。管理層會進行定期審
local environmental and other laws. Management 查以識別環境風險並確保制定的體系
performs regular reviews to identify environmental 足以管理該等風險。
risks and to ensure that the systems in place are
adequate to manage these risks.
ii. Climate, disease and other natural risks ii. 氣候、疾病及其他自然風險
The Group’s biological assets are exposed to the 本集團的生物資產面臨因氣候變化、
risk of damage from climatic changes, diseases 疾病及其他自然因素所產生的損害風
and other natural forces. The Group has extensive 險。本集團訂有詳盡的旨在監控及減
processes in place aimed at monitoring and 緩該等風險的流程，包括定期視察、
mitigating those risks, including regular inspections 疾病控制、調查及保險。
and disease controls and surveys and insurance.

The fair values of the financial assets and liabilities are 金融資產及負債之公允價值乃按有關工具在
included at the amount at which the instrument could be 交易雙方自願而非受脅迫或清盤銷售的情況
exchanged in a current transaction between willing parties, 下進行當前交易而轉手的金額入賬。下列方
other than in a forced or liquidation sale. The following 法及假設乃用於估計其公允價值：
methods and assumptions were used to estimate the fair
values:
The fair values of cash and cash equivalents, pledged 現金及現金等價物、已抵押存款及受限制現
deposits and restricted cash, trade receivables, deposits 金、應收款項、按金及其他應收款項、應付
and other receivables, trade payables, other payables and 款項、其他應付款項及應計項目以及短期計
accruals and short-term interest-bearing bank borrowings 息銀行借款之公允價值與其賬面值相若，主
approximate to their carrying amounts largely due to the 要由於該等工具的短期到期性質。
short-term maturities of these instruments.
The fair values of long-term receivable, long-term interest- 長期應收款項、長期計息銀行借款及租賃負
bearing bank borrowings and lease liabilities have been 債之公允價值已通過使用具有類似條款、信
calculated by discounting the expected future cash flows 貸風險及剩餘期限的工具當前可用的利率，
using rates currently available for instruments with similar 對預計未來現金流量進行貼現來計算。因本
terms, credit risk and remaining maturities. The changes in 集團自身於年底的長期應收款項、長期計息
fair value as a result of the Group’s own non-performance 銀行借款及租賃負債的不履約風險而導致的
risk for long-term receivable, long-term interest-bearing 公允價值變動已評估為並不重大。

Capital management 資本管理
The primary objectives of the Group’s capital management  本集團資本管理的主要目標為保障本集團持
are to safeguard the Group’s ability to continue as a going  續經營的能力，並維持穩健的資本比率，以
concern and to maintain healthy capital ratios in order to  支持其業務並最大化股東價值。
support its business and maximize shareholders’ value.
The Group manages its capital structure and makes  本集團管理其資本結構，並根據經濟狀況的
adjustments to it in light of changes in economic conditions  變化和相關資產的風險特徵對其進行調整。
and the risk characteristics of the underlying assets. To  為維持或調整資本結構，本集團可能會調整
maintain or adjust the capital structure, the Group may  向股東派付的股息、向股東退還資本或發行
新股。本集團不受任何外部施加的資本要求
adjust the dividend payment to shareholders, return capital
to shareholders or issue new shares. The Group is not  的規限。於有關年度，管理資本的目標、政
subject to any externally imposed capital requirements.  策或程序並無變動。
No changes were made in the objectives, policies or
processes for managing capital during the year.
The Group monitors capital using a gearing ratio, which  本集團使用資本負債比率（即債務除以資本）
is debt divided by capital. Debt includes interest-bearing  監控資本。債務包括計息銀行借款及租賃負
bank borrowings and lease liabilities. Capital includes  債。資本包括母公司擁有人應佔權益。於各
equity attributable to owners of the parent. The gearing  報告期末的資本負債比率如下：
ratios as at the end of each reporting period were as
